This is the ColorWay Sports NBA daily uniform tracker: what every team actually wore, every game, logged the morning after from game photos. The NBA publishes a uniform schedule before the season, but teams do not always follow it, so we check every game against it and flag the nights a club goes off script. Not just the City Editions and the throwbacks, the whole closet, Association whites, Icons and Statements included, because the patterns only show up when you keep the full record. Every matchup can also carry a ColorWay Sports letter grade for how the two jerseys look together.

Wondering what jersey your team is wearing tonight? The [2026-27 NBA uniform schedule](/stories/nba-uniform-schedule-2026-27) has every team's call for all 1,200 dated games, and each team's [uniform calendar](/nba-tracker) files every game it has played. This page is the receipts: what they actually put on.

## Sunday, October 25

7 games on Sunday, every jersey confirmed from game photos, and every team in what the NBA's schedule called. The headliner was in Brooklyn, the first Classic Edition against Classic Edition game of the season: the Pacers in their blue ABA Classic, a recreation of the 1971-74 road uniform, against the Nets in their white Classic with the red "Nets" script. Indiana won 136-109. The Knicks wore their black Statement at home and lost 111-102 to a Magic team in white, and the Grizzlies won 123-112 in Sacramento in their light blue Statement.

## Saturday, October 24

8 games on Saturday, all 16 jerseys exactly as scheduled. The best game was in Denver, where the Nuggets beat the Pelicans 141-135 in overtime, Denver in the navy Icon against New Orleans in white. The Wizards took their navy Statement on the road and won 124-120 in Chicago.

<!-- nba-day:2026-10-24:start -->

## Friday, October 23

12 games on Friday, the busiest night of the first week, and all 24 jerseys went to the NBA's schedule. This was the first big color-on-color night of the season, and every pairing held up: the Knicks' black Statement against the Celtics' green Icon in Boston, the Mavericks' navy "MAVS" Statement against the Pacers' gold pinstripes, the Rockets' red against the Spurs' black, and the Trail Blazers' red against the Kings' black.

<!-- nba-day:2026-10-23:start -->

<!-- nba-day:2026-10-22:end -->

## Wednesday, October 21

11 games on Wednesday, and the first schedule miss of the season. The NBA listed the Bulls in their Statement Edition in Toronto. Every Bulls game photo shows the white Association instead, against the Raptors' red Icon, and Toronto won 135-92. The Nets debuted their Classic Edition at home against the Hornets, and the Lakers opened in their gold Icon against the Warriors' royal blue. The full first week is in our [NBA opening week uniform roundup](/stories/nba-opening-week-uniforms-2026-27-what-every-team-wore).

<!-- nba-day:2026-10-21:start -->

## Tuesday, October 20

Opening night: 3 games, every jersey confirmed. The Knicks raised their championship banner in a one-night version of their home white with metallic gold lettering and a gold Champions patch, then lost 106-100 to the 76ers in royal blue. The Pistons came back from 22 points down to beat the Celtics, who wore their green Icon, and the Thunder won in San Antonio in their blue Icon.

<!-- nba-day:2026-10-20:start -->

## Frequently Asked Questions

**What are NBA teams wearing tonight?**

For tonight's games, the call comes from the NBA's official game-by-game uniform schedule, which lists both jerseys for every game. Our [NBA uniform schedule hub](/stories/nba-uniform-schedule-2026-27) links all 30 team schedules, and the homepage shows tonight's slate with both jerseys and our grade for each matchup. The morning after, this page shows what was actually worn.

**What did NBA teams wear last night?**

The newest night is always at the top of this page. Every game gets a card with both jerseys, the home team on the right, the final score and our matchup grade when we have one, logged the next morning once we have seen the game photos. Through the first six nights of 2026-27, 85 of the 86 jerseys matched the NBA's schedule.

**What jersey is my team wearing tonight?**

Open your team's 2026-27 uniform schedule from the [NBA uniform schedule hub](/stories/nba-uniform-schedule-2026-27). Each one lists the full closet and the edition for every dated game, from the Association white to the City Edition nights. Each team's [uniform calendar](/nba-tracker) shows the same season on a calendar, with the jersey worn in every game already played.

**Do NBA teams wear white at home?**

Not always. Since 2017-18 the NBA has let the home team choose its jersey, and the visitor wears whatever contrasts. The white Association is still the most-worn jersey in the league, but home teams wear color often: on opening week the home team wore color in most games through the first two nights.

**What is the difference between the Association, Icon, Statement, City and Classic Editions?**

The Association Edition is each team's white jersey. The Icon Edition is its main color jersey. The Statement Edition is a second color jersey, often a darker or bolder look. The City Edition is a one-season design tied to the team's city, and the Classic Edition brings back a throwback from the team's history.

**Do teams always wear what the NBA's uniform schedule says?**

Almost always, but not every time. On October 21 the schedule had the Bulls in their Statement Edition at Toronto, and every game photo showed them in the white Association. That is exactly what this tracker is for: we check every game against the schedule and mark the misses.

**How do you know what each team wore?**

We check the team's own game photos, the NBA's team galleries and the game highlights the morning after, and we confirm both jerseys before anything is logged. A game we have not seen does not get a card. Scores are the official finals.

**Where can I see every game a team has played this season?**

On its [team uniform calendar](/nba-tracker). This page keeps the five most recent nights; the team pages keep the whole season, with the jersey from every confirmed game and running home and road counts.

## The Bottom Line on the NBA Daily Uniform Tracker

The NBA has the biggest closets in sports, five or six jerseys a team, and a schedule that is right almost every night. This page catches the nights it is not, the City and Classic debuts as they arrive, and every standard night in between, so the record is complete through June. Check it with your morning coffee, and for what your team wears next, the [NBA uniform schedule](/stories/nba-uniform-schedule-2026-27) has the dates.
